# Skills · R · part 36

887 entries, recall to recap. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **recall**: Search the shared eidetic memory store and get back ranked, provenanced records. Drives `eidetic recall` with four search modes — exact (verbatim substring), approximate (vector/semantic), keyword (BM25 lexical), and hybrid (a weighted blend of vector+keyword, the default) — each hit carrying its text, full metadata, a relevance `score`, and a freshness `signal`. Recall passively reinforces matched records (bumps last_recall + recall_count). Shadowed and archived records are excluded by default; use --include-shadowed / --include-archived to retrieve them. The store lives at ~/.eidetic/memory… ([agentculture/org](https://github.com/agentculture/org/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/recall) · ★2)

- **recall**: Recall from brethof-brain memory — current truth first, full conversation history second. Use before asking the user something that may already be answered. Trigger on: recall, remember, what did we decide, look it up, check memory. - **recall**: Answer "what do I know about X" by searching the whole spine (brain/, memory/, projects/, desks/) and past session transcripts with ripgrep, following [[wikilinks]], and synthesizing a cited answer with a staleness note. No database and no embeddings: disciplined search plus the model doing the synthesis. Use when you say "/recall X", "what do I know about X", "what's the latest on X", or "pull everything on X". ([brookejlacey/lane-os](https://github.com/brookejlacey/lane-os/tree/HEAD/skills/recall) · ★2)

- **recall**: Rebuild the current state of prior work from local agent history, saved Dmnkstack state, git, and linked work records. ([dmnkgrc/dotfiles](https://github.com/dmnkgrc/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/plugins/dmnkstack/skills/recall) · ★2)
- **recall**: Find a past session when the user is lost: "where did I do that thing", "did I ever", "have we done X", "find that session", "I'm lost", "what was that thing", "which repo was that in". Greps the recall index (D:\worktrees\history\INDEX.tsv) first, then the recap bodies, then transcripts only if needed, and returns the matching sessions with their worktree path, recap file, and transcript id. This is the read side of the recap skill: recap writes the index, recall reads it. It locates work; it does not modify anything. ([dovholuknf/dotfiles](https://github.com/dovholuknf/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/claude/skills/recall) · ★2)
- **recall**: Retrieve stored knowledge. Recall uses hybrid search (vector + full-text + graph expansion) by default — it is the most comprehensive retrieval mode. ([dpoage/known](https://github.com/dpoage/known/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/recall) · ★2 · 2 copies)

- **recall**: Use when the friend explicitly wants to look something up in the wiki mid-session, beyond what wake-up already injected. - **recall**: Semantic recall across the whole workspace - Odin brain, threads, CRM, context, plans, outputs, knowledge and auto-memory - answering only from retrieved sources with file citations, or saying "not in memory". Use for "what do we know about X", "where did we decide Y", "have we touched Z before". - **recall**: Припоминание записанного: «мы это решали?», «мы обсуждали», «по моим заметкам», «что у нас с этим», «где записано», «найди в графе», recall. Также перед вопросом человеку о потенциально записанном, после сжатия контекста, посреди кода, когда нужно основание решения. Лестница: поиск → связи → где должно лежать → брат → человек; найденное — с источником и потолком. Не нынешняя воля человека, не вход в граф (entry), не личная память (minding). Нужны искрон-тулы. ([iskron-ai/skills](https://github.com/iskron-ai/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/recall) · ★2)
- **recall**: Proactive memory exploration. Déclencheurs : /recall, « où j'en étais ? », « remets-moi dans le bain », « qu'est-ce que j'ai fait sur X ? », « catch me up », « reprends le sujet X ». ([Warshoow/agent-setup](https://github.com/Warshoow/agent-setup/tree/HEAD/skills/recall))
- **recall**: Recall command output that the Jev output ladder hid, or ask Jev a bounded choice / score / yes-no question. Use when a Bash result ends with a "[jev] ... hidden ranges" footer, or when a decision has a known set of answers. ([weiping/jev-claude-code](https://github.com/weiping/jev-claude-code/tree/HEAD/plugins/jev/skills/recall))
- **recall**: Use when the user wants to recall what they or an agent did in a prior session, search past Codex sessions for a task or decision, trace how a task concluded or was corrected, or check session provenance; strictly read-only, never writes or promotes session claims. ([wu8685/me](https://github.com/wu8685/me/tree/HEAD/skills/recall))
- **recall**: Search hinddian long-term memory for a question. Trigger: /hinddian:recall &lt;question&gt; ([wyq0x55/hinddian](https://github.com/wyq0x55/hinddian/tree/HEAD/plugins/hinddian/skills/recall))
- **recall**: Cross-session artifact index. Find and read outputs from previous sessions — reviews, op strategy results, plans/PRDs, eval scores, probe verdicts — stored under .xm/. Use when the user asks to recall/find/look up a past review/op/plan, "what did the last review say", "show me the previous op result", "pull up the last plan", or wants a prior artifact handed to another tool/session (Codex, Cursor). Also exposes a tool-neutral handoff (HANDOFF.md). ([x-mesh/xm](https://github.com/x-mesh/xm/tree/HEAD/x-recall/skills/recall) · 2 copies)
- **recall**: Store and retrieve path-, command-, or topic-triggered micro-rules in &lt;1ms using bundled Rust SQLite FTS5/BM25 instead of bloating AGENTS.md or MEMORY.md. Injects only the 1-3 matching facts (~120 tokens). ([xgbtxy/ap](https://github.com/xgbtxy/ap/tree/HEAD/skills/recall))
- **recall**: プロジェクト固有の質問を受けたとき、このスキルで過去の決定事項を先に検索する。「〜はどうなった？」「〜だっけ？」「〜でしたっけ？」「なぜ〜を使っている？」「このプロジェクトでは〜」のような質問に対して、推測ではなく記録に基づいて回答する。一般的な技術知識には不要。「保存して」「メモして」「記録して」は→ remember。セッション作業状態の復元は→ agent-memory。 ([yAtomtom/dotfiles](https://github.com/yAtomtom/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/recall) · 2 copies)
- **recall**: Run a cold-recall interview drill on topics from study/tracker.md — 八股, fundamentals, post-training theory, or "defend your own project". Use when the user says "quiz me", "recall session", "test me on X", "八股", "ask me interview questions", or wants to convert reading into a passed gate. Asks interview-hard questions with no scaffolding, grades the answer, and proposes a status change. Does NOT teach, and does NOT find or archive material. ([yifeiwu12138/skills](https://github.com/yifeiwu12138/skills/tree/HEAD/recall))
